The MafE-MafH-MafD pathway is an alternative pathway to the classical naphthoate pathway for menaquinone biosynthesis. In this pathway:
- MafE converts 1,4-dihydroxy-2-naphthoyl-CoA to aminodeoxyfutalosine (ADFS)
- MafH (or MafH2) deaminates aminodeoxyfutalosine to futalosine
- MafD converts futalosine to 1,4-dihydroxy-2-naphthoate (DHNA)

The signature motif for MafH includes the &quot;TCPRSN&quot; and &quot;DWPAFAAAG&quot; sequences which are clearly visible in this protein.

This protein appears to be a subtilisin-like alkaline protease (possibly subtilisin Carlsberg or a related alkaline protease), and the most precise reported optimum would be pH 9.0.
